- **msi-annotation-fdr-filtering**: Use when selecting usable public METASPACE imaging MS datasets for annotation or benchmarking, or when comparing METASPACE-ML vs rule-based MSM annotations against MS/MS-validated LC-MS/MS ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-annotation-fdr-filtering))
- **msi-annotation-level-assignment**: Use when significant m/z hits from statistical analysis (e.g. a t-test on DI-MS/LC-MS peak tables) need putative metabolite annotations with MSI confidence levels after a compound-database m/z search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-annotation-level-assignment))
- **msi-annotation-level-classification**: Use when a feature table with database annotation matches (annotate_tbl) needs per-feature MSI confidence levels — e.g. to separate metabolites with m/z+RT+MS2 support from those with m/z-only matches before reporting or filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-annotation-level-classification--b930b2756f8db272))
- **msi-annotation-level-classification**: Use when MS/MS spectra (e.g. from a microbeMASST or MASST batch search) need putative metabolite annotations, specifically when library matches and in-silico class predictions must be mapped to 2007 MSI annotation levels (e.g. level 2) and summarized as an annotation rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-annotation-level-classification--d21ce8aa49985cc5))
- **msi-array-data-structure-handling**: Use when you have imported raw imzML or Analyze 7.5 imaging data and need to represent it as a preprocessed spectral array before applying statistical methods (PCA, clustering, classification). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-array-data-structure-handling))
- **msi-batch-installer**: Install a set of .msi files silently via msiexec with logging, exit-code decoding, and summary reporting. Provides both CMD batch and PowerShell scripts. ([binrogithub/1-3-Cloud-Adoption-Skills](https://github.com/binrogithub/1-3-Cloud-Adoption-Skills/tree/HEAD/AI-Assist-Migration/Workspace-Migration/msi-batch-installer) · ★14)
- **msi-ce-ms-data-preprocessing**: Use when preprocessing large batches of targeted full-scan MSI-CE-MS data (mzML files) where large migration time fluctuations across batches make manual vendor-software peak annotation/integration prohibitively slow, and accurate peak annotation, integration, and sample position assignment are. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ce-ms-data-preprocessing))
- **msi-ce-ms-metabolite-peak-annotation**: Use when preprocessing targeted full-scan MSI-CE-MS mzML files that require peak annotation, peak integration, and sample position assignment despite large migration time variability between runs, batches, or months of acquisition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ce-ms-metabolite-peak-annotation))
- **msi-ce-ms-migration-index-annotation**: Use when pre-processing targeted full-scan MSI-CE-MS mzML files with high migration-time variability, where peak annotation must be automated across multiplexed injections and thousands of serum samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ce-ms-migration-index-annotation))
- **msi-ce-ms-peak-annotation**: Use when pre-processing targeted full-scan multiplexed MSI-CE-MS mzML runs where large migration-time variability makes standard alignment tools unsuitable and metabolite peaks must be annotated, integrated, and assigned to serial injection positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ce-ms-peak-annotation))
- **msi-ce-ms-sample-position-assignment**: Use when pre-processing multiplexed MSI-CE-MS runs (multiple serial injections per run) and each detected peak must be mapped back to the injected sample position it originated from, with migration times that drift batch-to-batch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ce-ms-sample-position-assignment))
- **msi-composite-image-upsampling**: Use when an MSI cluster/composite image must be resampled to the pixel dimensions of a co-registered H&E image, and the quality/speed tradeoff between interpolation methods needs to be assessed or reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-composite-image-upsampling))
- **msi-confidence-level-annotation**: Use when annotating untargeted LC-MS/MS metabolomics spectra (e.g. urine on HILIC/CSH Q Exactive HF) with tiered MSI confidence levels 1-3, or when quantifying the annotated vs unannotated fraction of MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-confidence-level-annotation))
- **msi-confidence-level-annotation-interpretation**: Use when you need metabolite annotation for LC-MS features using accurate mass, isotopic patterns, and MS^E fragmentation spectra at MSI confidence level 2, or when annotated features are required as ground truth to validate feature-matching results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-confidence-level-annotation-interpretation))
- **msi-data-format-interoperability**: Use when your MSI data is stored in a Cardinal imaging experiment object (version 2.2+) that has already been peak-binned with peakBin(), and you want to run mass2adduct's massdiff() and adductMatch() pipeline without manually exporting to CSV; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/msi-data-format-interoperability) · 2 copies)
- **msi-data-import-csv**: Use when you have MSI intensity data exported from MSiReader or SCiLS software as a plain-text CSV file (with peaks as columns and pixels/spots as rows), and you need to import it into R to perform pairwise mass difference calculations and adduct identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-data-import-csv))
- **msi-data-matrix-import-and-validation**: Use when you have MSI intensity data exported from MSiReader, SCiLS, or Cardinal as plain-text CSV files or as Cardinal MSProcessedImagingExperiment/MSContinuousImagingExperiment objects, and need to load it into R as a validated msimat object for mass difference and adduct analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-data-matrix-import-and-validation) · 3 copies)
- **msi-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw .imzML/.ibd MSI pixel data (e.g. test_POS from MetaboLights MTBLS487) must be converted into a filtered, deisotoped ion feature table before lipid annotation at a chosen ppm.annotate tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-data-preprocessing))
- **msi-data-processing-speed-measurement**: Use when when you need to validate that MSI software (e.g., LipidQMap) achieves documented processing speeds on your target hardware, or when you need to establish a performance baseline before deploying the software for high-throughput imaging studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-data-processing-speed-measurement))
- **msi-data-quality-benchmarking**: Use when you have reconstructed high-resolution FT-ICR MSI spectra (e.g. MEISTER output) alongside raw low-resolution transients processed by traditional FT, and need to quantify SNR improvement per peak to validate that deep-learning reconstruction is viable before downstream 3D mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-data-quality-benchmarking))
- **msi-data-table-export**: Use when after calculating mean intensity values across all spectra in an MSI dataset (or within a manually selected ROI), and you need to store the resulting m/z–intensity table in a portable format for downstream ROI analysis, database annotation, or external statistical pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-data-table-export))
- **msi-data-visualization-and-spatial-analysis**: Use when when you have imzML-format MSI data and need to identify biochemical species in specific tissue regions or anatomical structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-data-visualization-and-spatial-analysis))
- **msi-detection**: Microsatellite Instability (MSI) detection workflows — msisensor-pro usage, baseline construction, threshold determination, anomaly detection, cancer-type stratification. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/msi-detection))
- **msi-detection-analysis**: MSI (Microsatellite Instability) detection analysis pipeline — collecting results from msisensor-pro/TopMSI, threshold determination, per-cancer stratification, ROC evaluation, and prediction on unlabeled cohorts. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/msi-detection-analysis))
- **msi-detection-pipeline**: Microsatellite Instability (MSI) detection from NGS data — baseline construction, threshold determination, result aggregation, and cancer-type-stratified analysis. Covers msisensor-pro and custom TopMSI pipelines. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/msi-detection-pipeline))
- **msi-feature-matrix-regional-group-assignment**: Use when a spatial metabolomics feature matrix (pixels x m/z ion peaks) needs pixel-level regional grouping before Wilcoxon/FC-based differential comparison, e.g. benchmarking SMAnalyst against Cardinal-defined anatomical clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-feature-matrix-regional-group-assignment))
- **msi-feature-metadata-preservation**: Use when you are converting a processed Cardinal MSImagingExperiment object (containing normalized peaks, optional spatial shrunken centroids segmentation, and feature m/z annotations) into a Seurat object for downstream pathway analysis, differential metabolite expression, or integrative. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-feature-metadata-preservation) · 2 copies)
- **msi-he-image-overlay-compositing**: Use when imzML MSI peptide ion images and a registered/binarized H&E histology image must be merged into per-protein overlay panels, e.g. after co-registration and LC-MS/MS peptide filtering are already done. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-he-image-overlay-compositing))
- **msi-hyperspectral-datacube-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a reconstructed serial-section 3D MSI dataset (e.g. an m/z-binned, TIC-normalized .h5/.imzML volume) must be converted into grayscale feature images that show anatomical contrast for image registration against MRI/atlas slices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-hyperspectral-datacube-dimensionality-reduction))
- **msi-hyperspectral-spectra-loading**: Use when pixel-level mass spectra must be brought from imzML (exported from SCiLS lab) or Metabolomics Workbench into HDF5 and TIC-normalized, without peak picking, as input to a deep learning classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-hyperspectral-spectra-loading))
- **msi-imaging-experiment-class-manipulation**: Use when when loading mass spectrometry imaging data from imzML or Analyze 7. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-imaging-experiment-class-manipulation))
- **msi-installments**: Implement Mexico MSI or fixed installment payments with Ecart Pay — tokenize and create orders with installment fields. Use when the user mentions MSI, meses sin intereses, installments, or cuota. ([envia-ep/ecartpay-plugin](https://github.com/envia-ep/ecartpay-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/ecart-pay/skills/msi-installments))
- **msi-intensity-matrix-isotope-correction**: Use when you have loaded a feature-by-pixel intensity matrix (HDF5 format following Cardinal::HDF5 layout) from imzML MSI data in positive ion mode and you have identified paired [M+H]+ and [M+Na]+ features for the same neutral lipid species (differing by ~22 Da in m/z). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-intensity-matrix-isotope-correction))
- **msi-intensity-matrix-normalization**: Use when after isotope correction when you have extracted intensity matrices from imzML or HDF5 MSI data and need to convert raw or isotope-corrected ion-image intensities into quantified values using a known internal standard lipid species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-intensity-matrix-normalization))
- **msi-intensity-normalisation-and-pareto-scaling**: Use when an imzML-derived, annotated MSI feature table (e.g. image.norm.csv from massPix) must be prepared as input for PCA or clustering, and pixel-to-pixel intensity variation needs correction before scaling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-intensity-normalisation-and-pareto-scaling))
- **msi-intensity-normalization**: Use when raw MSI data (e.g., rat brain sections, MALDI) must be converted into preprocessed 2D pixel×ion intensity matrices—e.g., before DeepION representation learning or other ion-colocalization tasks requiring uniform [0,1] intensities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-intensity-normalization))
- **msi-ion-image-augmentation**: Use when training a contrastive encoder on MSI ion images and you need augmentation pairs that reflect MSI priors — Poisson ion counts, missing values, or isotope-ion relationships — rather than generic image transforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ion-image-augmentation))
- **msi-ion-image-extraction-and-overlay-visualization**: Use when you have a preprocessed imzML dataset and need to extract an ion image at a specific m/z, or to overlay that ion image on a co-registered H&E stain for spatial localization review. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-ion-image-extraction-and-overlay-visualization))
- **msi-laptop-drivers**: Install and manage drivers for MSI laptops on Arch Linux. Covers networking (Killer E-series), fan/thermal management, embedded controller access, and NVIDIA GPU settings. Triggers: MSI laptop, MSI Titan, MSI Stealth, MSI Raider, MSI Vector, MSI Crosshair, MSI Summit, msi-ec, r8125, coolercontrol, Killer E3000, MSI fan control, MSI EC, nvidia-settings. ([asimov-agent/llgenie](https://github.com/asimov-agent/llgenie/tree/HEAD/agents/llgenie-planner/skills/.archive/msi-laptop-drivers) · ★1)
- **msi-level1-authentic-standard-validation**: Use when you have MSI level 1 authentic-standard MS/MS identifications and need to quantify how accurately an in silico annotation tool (e.g., CSI:FingerID) ranks the true structure among its candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-level1-authentic-standard-validation))
- **msi-matrix-reshaping-and-shape-validation**: Use when raw MSI matrices must be exported as a two-dimensional pixel-by-ion matrix plus a one-dimensional peak list and the horizontal/vertical pixel shape passed to DeepION's --input_shape must be verified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-matrix-reshaping-and-shape-validation))
- **msi-metabolite-annotation**: Use when you have preprocessed MSI data with a measured m/z column and need to assign metabolite names and adduct forms by matching against an organ- and ion-mode-specific metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-metabolite-annotation))
- **msi-metabolite-database-interpretation**: Use when metabolite annotations from ambient MSI (e.g., AFADESI-MSI of mouse body sections) must be organized into per-organ membership/enrichment sets and compared across organs, ionization modes, or mouse strains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-metabolite-database-interpretation))
- **msi-metadata-curation-and-storage**: Use when after generating a 3D pixel array (shape n+1, y, x) from raw line-scan MSI data via MSIGen's get_image_data() call, curate and validate the accompanying JSON metadata file before visualization or downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-metadata-curation-and-storage) · 2 copies)
- **msi-metadata-harmonization-across-image-stacks**: Use when you have processed and quantified MSI data from one or more imzML files in LipidQMap and need to export them as a unified, standards-compliant HDF5 container that preserves feature-by-pixel intensity matrices, per-feature lipid annotations (m/z, lipid class, adduct, neutral ID), per-pixel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-metadata-harmonization-across-image-stacks))
- **msi-mz-matching**: Use when a preprocessed MSI feature list (measured m/z in column 1) must be annotated against an organ-specific metabolite database (metabolite name + theoretical m/z in specified adduct columns) with a controlled ppm tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-mz-matching))
- **msi-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a centroid-mode MSI peak matrix (e.g. rMSIproc .zip format) and need dimensionality reduction to visually compare data quality before and after a peak-removal step such as matrix-related signal removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-pca-dimensionality-reduction))
- **msi-peak-annotation**: Use when you have processed MSI data (peak matrix and spatial coordinates) from matrix-assisted laser desorption/ionization (MALDI) or silver-assisted laser desorption/ionization (AgLDI) experiments, and you need to computationally distinguish matrix ions from analyte ions before downstream feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-peak-annotation))
- **msi-peak-filtering-pooling**: Use when raw MALDI-MSI section data (e.g. rat brain, positive or negative ion mode) must be converted into a validated pixel-by-ion intensity matrix with a paired peak list before running DeepION COL/ISO or similar ion-image representation models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-filtering-pooling))
- **msi-peak-intensity-extraction**: Use when you have MSI intensity data exported from commercial software (MSiReader, SCiLS) or Cardinal processing pipelines as plain-text CSV files or Cardinal MSProcessedImagingExperiment/MSContinuousImagingExperiment objects, and you need to prepare it for mass-difference tabulation and adduct. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-peak-intensity-extraction))
- **msi-peak-matrix-processing**: Use when raw MSI spectral data (imzML in profile mode) must be converted into a centroid-mode peak matrix and processed dataset suitable for matrix-related peak annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-matrix-processing))
- **msi-peak-pair-mass-difference-calculation**: Use when you have binned MSI peak data (CSV from SCiLS/MSiReader or an msimat object) and need to enumerate candidate adduct ion pairs by mass difference before adduct annotation and spatial correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-pair-mass-difference-calculation))
- **msi-peak-picked-intensity-analysis**: Use when segmenting peak-picked MSI pixel-by-feature intensity data (e.g. processed imzML loaded into Cardinal) into spatially coherent tissue regions and selecting the number of segments by scanning a regularization parameter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-picked-intensity-analysis))
- **msi-peak-picking-alignment**: Use when raw MALDI MSI sections must be converted into a common peak list and a [X*Y,P] ion-intensity matrix with normalized intensities before a representation model such as DeepION is trained. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-picking-alignment))
- **msi-peak-table-import-msimat**: Use when starting a mass2adduct adduct-analysis workflow on MALDI-MSI data and you need to load exported peak tables or processed Cardinal objects into the `msimat` class before calling massdiff, adductMatch, or corrPairsMSI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-peak-table-import-msimat))
- **msi-pixel-classification-by-ifm-marker**: Use when co-registered MALDI MSI and immunofluorescence microscopy data are available and you need to assign lipids to marker-defined cell populations (e.g. Ly6G+ neutrophils in UPEC-infected bladder) via supervised pixel classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-pixel-classification-by-ifm-marker))
- **msi-pixel-filtering**: Use when a spatial metabolomics peak table matrix (pixels x m/z ion columns) contains off-tissue/background pixels that must be removed before downstream clustering, e.g. when an intensity image shows clearly separable background signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-pixel-filtering))
- **msi-pixel-to-spot-binning**: Use when pairing pixel-resolution MALDI imaging mass spectrometry data with spot-based ST data (e.g. Visium) from the same tissue section, and a shared spot-level object is needed for multi-omic integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msi-pixel-to-spot-binning))
- **msi-preprocessing-parameter-configuration**: Use when you have raw line-scan MSI data (from Bruker .d/.baf, converted to .mzML, or other supported formats) and need to configure MSIGen before calling msigen() to generate image arrays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msi-preprocessing-parameter-configuration) · 2 copies)
- **msi-principal-component-analysis**: Use when the input is an MSI dataset (imzML or Analyze 7.5) and the goal is unsupervised exploratory analysis to discover regions of interest, or when benchmarking the runtime cost of PCA on raw versus pre-processed/peak-picked spectra. - **msms-exclusion-list-generation**: Use when PeakBot has annotated walls/background signals in blank or background LC-HRMS samples and you need a targeted DDA exclusion list to boost MS/MS coverage of true chromatographic peaks in subsequent runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-exclusion-list-generation))
- **msms-feature-detect**: Detect and align LC-MS features with GNPS2 Everything Bagel or local MZmine, then export feature MS/MS spectra for structure elucidation. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-feature-detect) · ★3)
- **msms-feature-preprocessing**: Use when a .mgf file of MS/MS feature spectra (e.g. wheat or urine metabolome LC–MS/MS with &lt;1000–&lt;4000 features) must be turned into a specXplore session data object with pairwise similarity matrices before t-SNE or dashboard exploration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-feature-preprocessing))
- **msms-fingerprint-compare**: Compare MIST-predicted fingerprints against deterministic Morgan fingerprints for the same molecules via Tanimoto similarity. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-fingerprint-compare) · ★3)
- **msms-fingerprint-prediction-evaluation**: Use when a study has MS/MS spectra left unannotated by spectral library search (e.g. IDSL.CSA vs NIST 20/LipidBlast) and you need to quantify annotation gains from predicted-fingerprint structure matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fingerprint-prediction-evaluation))
- **msms-formula-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when you have ranked candidate molecular formulas per MS/MS spectrum (from FSA or a comparable ranker) and need to quantify rank#1 accuracy, top-5 accuracy, and per-spectrum runtime against known metabolite ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-formula-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **msms-fragment-annotation**: Use when you need theoretical MS/MS fragment ions for derivatized unsaturated sterols (or other C=C-containing molecules) to support matching of LC-IM-MS/MS features against a reference lipid database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-annotation))
- **msms-fragment-bag-of-words-corpus-construction**: Use when raw MS2 spectra (e.g. from beer-extract or other complex-mixture LC-MS/MS runs) must be converted into the fragment-and-neutral-loss bag-of-words documents that LDA/MS2LDA topic modeling consumes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-bag-of-words-corpus-construction))
- **msms-fragment-binning-and-factorization**: Use when comparing MS/MS spectra of several features from an .mgf file to see which fragment m/z values and neutral losses they share in one heatmap view. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-binning-and-factorization))
- **msms-fragment-data-extraction**: Use when UNIFI-exported .xlsx MS/MS spectra of reference standards need to be converted into a fragment datatable stratified by standard and collision energy (20/40/60 eV). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-data-extraction))
- **msms-fragment-intensity-extraction**: Use when quantifying histone peptides from Mascot .csv output by MS/MS total ion intensity, especially when composite spectra contain two isobaric peptides sharing the same sequence but distinct PTM localizations that must be deconvoluted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/msms-fragment-intensity-extraction))
- **msms-fragment-ion-overlap-quantification**: Use when LC–MS/MS spectra (.mgf) are embedded via an ms2deepscore similarity matrix and you need to characterize how much of the fragmentation signal is shared among spectra in a dense local neighborhood around a feature of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-ion-overlap-quantification))
- **msms-fragment-ion-prediction**: Use when public/commercial MS/MS libraries lack spectra for a compound class (e.g., &lt;50 acylcarnitine structures) and MSI level 2 annotation requires predicted fragment ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-ion-prediction))
- **msms-fragment-lfp-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have chained MS/MS fragment ion-intensity profiles of two (or more) isomeric compounds across multiple collision energies and need per-fragment similarity scores to distinguish them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-lfp-similarity-scoring))
- **msms-fragment-library-generation**: Reference for building or extending metabolite fragment libraries when new experimental MS/MS spectra (e.g. MassBank records such as MSBNK-RIKEN-PR100295 for Pantothenic acid [M+H]+) must be converted into scored .csv entries for MetaboAnnotatoR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-library-generation))
- **msms-fragment-library-searching**: Use when an aligned feature table contains m/z and retention-time pairs whose monoisotopic precursor masses need compound-level annotation via MS/MS spectral matching against an online MassBank library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-library-searching))
- **msms-fragment-mass-enumeration**: Use when building targeted PRM/MRM or DIA assays for lipids and you must enumerate precursor-to-fragment transitions (m/z, adduct, charge) for lipid classes rather than measuring them experimentally. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-mass-enumeration))
- **msms-fragment-overlap-analysis**: Decision support for whether CFM-ID predicted fragments can back annotation of DNA adducts in HRMS adductomics when experimental MS/MS spectra exist only for a subset (e.g. 15 of 582) of database entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-overlap-analysis))
- **msms-fragment-pattern-search**: Use when you have centroided LC/MS data (mzML) with a feature table and MS2 scans, and a known diagnostic product ion (e.g., m/z 333.0592) to screen for structurally related features across many spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-pattern-search))
- **msms-fragment-peak-matching**: Use when annotating an LC-MS/MS peak (e.g. negative-mode peak M386T77, m/z 385.1118, RT 1.27 min) by matching its fragment peaks to a candidate metabolite's reference fragments within a 10 ppm tolerance to obtain an MS/MS score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-peak-matching))
- **msms-fragment-profile-matching**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS data are acquired at multiple collision energies (e.g., stepped NCE 20/40/60%) and isomers with similar substructures must be distinguished, so a single cross-sectional spectrum match is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-profile-matching))
- **msms-fragment-screening**: Use when a blank-filtered LC-HRMS/MS feature table (e.g. AFFF samples) needs PFAS candidate flagging via MS/MS fragment matching, especially for precursor ions whose exact masses match nothing in the EPA catalogued PFAS list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-screening))
- **msms-fragment-simulation-for-n-me-derivatized-sterols**: Use when building a spectral library or 4D identification database for N-Me derivatized unsaturated sterol lipids, and theoretical fragment ions matching N-Me fragmentation patterns are needed for LC-IM-MS/MS annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragment-simulation-for-n-me-derivatized-sterols))
- **msms-fragmentation-candidate-ranking**: Use when a challenge provides MS/MS peak lists (possibly merged across collision energies) with a known elemental composition, and candidate structures must be retrieved from PubChem and ranked without reference spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragmentation-candidate-ranking))
- **msms-fragmentation-depth-sweep**: Use when you need to assess how deeply MAGMa breadth-first fragmentation (depth 2 vs 3 vs 4) affects ModiFinder's shifted-peak annotation coverage and MF-O evaluation scores on an MS/MS analog pair benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragmentation-depth-sweep))
- **msms-fragmentation-pattern-annotation**: Use when a GNPS molecular network (MS/MS spectra as vertices, modified-cosine edges) must be interpreted by mapping mineMS2-mined fragmentation patterns onto its components and exporting for Cytoscape. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragmentation-pattern-annotation))
- **msms-fragmentation-pattern-matching-to-network-components**: Use when you have a mined mineMS2 pattern library plus a GNPS molecular network (components of similar MS/MS spectra) and need to identify which patterns specifically explain each network component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-fragmentation-pattern-matching-to-network-components))
- **msms-hypergeometric-score-histogram-analysis**: Use when you have per-feature MS/MS spectral match scores from searching lipid annotations against two or more spectral libraries (e.g., CalicoLipids vs MS-DIAL) and need library-specific score cutoffs to separate authentic from spurious identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-hypergeometric-score-histogram-analysis))
- **msms-inspect**: Interactive TIC/MS1/MS2 chromatogram viewer for mzML files; lets you browse retention time, inspect precursor masses, and export individual spectra as .ms files. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-inspect) · ★3)
- **msms-intensity-coincidence-analysis**: Use when calibrating an electronic-noise removal threshold for MS/MS spectra: you need to empirically justify how many ions per spectrum can share near-identical relative intensities (±0.1%) before denoising experimental Orbitrap data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-intensity-coincidence-analysis))
- **msms-intensity-coverage-filtering**: Use when curating raw MS/MS spectral libraries (NIST'17, MS-DIAL, MSnLib .mgf/.msp entries) into training-ready data for in silico fragmentation models and uncurated spectra must be quality-controlled by fragment-to-peak matching criteria. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-intensity-coverage-filtering))
- **msms-intensity-normalization-to-unit-sum**: Use when a centroided MS/MS spectrum has been cleaned (peaks merged within +/- 0.05 Da, noise-filtered) and you need intensities as a probability distribution for spectral entropy or entropy-similarity calculations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-intensity-normalization-to-unit-sum))
- **msms-intensity-weighting**: Use when a centroided, normalized MS/MS peak list must be intensity-weighted prior to entropy similarity computation and the spectrum's entropy (or ion richness) is unknown or low (S &lt; 3). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-intensity-weighting))
- **msms-library-count-verification**: Use when a deposited MS/MS reference library (MGF or matchms-readable format) carries reported totals of spectra and unique compounds that must be independently verified against the Zenodo archive before downstream use or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-library-count-verification))
- **msms-library-matching**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS feature tables from lipidomics experiments (e.g. Thermo .raw, SCIEX .wiff, .mzML inputs) need MS2 library-search annotation against a theoretical spectral library covering lipid chain compositions and C=C double-bond positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-library-matching))
- **msms-longitudinal-fragment-profiling-scoring**: Use when annotating metabolites from MS/MS spectra acquired at multiple distinct collision energies (e.g., stepped NCE 20/40/60%) and you need per-candidate similarity ranking plus ROC-derived identification thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-longitudinal-fragment-profiling-scoring))
- **msms-metadata-harmonization**: Use when a raw or merged MS/MS spectral library in MGF format contains inconsistent metadata fields, missing precursor m/z or ionization mode, or annotations whose parent mass/adduct contradict the precursor m/z and SMILES. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-metadata-harmonization))
- **msms-mist-fingerprint**: Predict molecular fingerprints from MS/MS spectra using only FRIGID's MIST encoder, skipping diffusion generation and ICEBERG refinement. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-mist-fingerprint) · ★3)
- **msms-modified-cosine-similarity-scoring**: Use when building the testing matrix of MS/MS fingerprints for NPOmix KNN classification — i.e., when you have query reference spectra (e.g., 15 GNPS reference spectra) and paired samples with merged experimental spectra, and need per-sample maximum modified cosine similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-modified-cosine-similarity-scoring))
- **msms-modified-cosine-spectral-matching**: Use when you have paired genome–metabolome samples (PoDP-style, e.g. the 1,040-sample GNPS job task=b915d1f78c364bf4be9900047760d95c) and reference or cryptic MS/MS spectra that must be converted into similarity fingerprints for BGC–metabolite linking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-modified-cosine-spectral-matching))
- **msms-molecular-networking**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS metabolomics files (e.g. mzML/MassIVE MSV000083738) must be grouped into molecular families to identify analogues of known metabolites such as detoxins and rimosamides. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-molecular-networking))
- **msms-multiple-spectral-alignment-across-molecular-family**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra grouped into a GNPS2 molecular network component (or a set of USIs) and need to see which peaks align across all spectra — including shifted analog peaks like rhamnolipid HAA fragments (m/z 211.130 vs 239. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-multiple-spectral-alignment-across-molecular-family))
- **msms-neutral-loss-annotation**: Use when a filtered feature table contains a candidate metabolite feature (e.g. m/z 185.1183) whose identity must be supported by MS/MS evidence such as a diagnostic neutral loss before accepting it in the final metabolite set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-neutral-loss-annotation))
- **msms-neutral-loss-interpretation**: Use when interpreting ICEBERG fragment attribution outputs — e.g., after running predict_smis.py on held-out molecules — to check whether breakage frequencies and hydrogen shifts match chemistry intuition (C-O/C-N favored over C-C; -1H ↔ H2O loss). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-neutral-loss-interpretation))
- **msms-noise-denoising**: Use when raw MS/MS query spectra (e.g., AD plasma from Orbitrap Astral or Exploris 240) contain electronic and chemical noise ions, and the goal is higher-confidence compound annotation via spectral library matching with entropy similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-noise-denoising))
- **msms-noise-filtering**: Use when raw MS2 fragmentation spectra paired to MS1 peaks contain noisy fragment or neutral-loss entries that must be removed before building a bag-of-fragments matrix or running topic-model inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-noise-filtering))
- **msms-noise-ion-simulation**: Use when benchmarking MS/MS library-matching similarity scores for robustness: you have cleaned, centroided reference spectra and need to quantify score degradation as artificial noise ions are added at graded levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-noise-ion-simulation))
- **msms-pairwise-similarity-computation**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra (MGF files) from a spectral library or extract library and need pairwise similarity scores that reflect structural relatedness, e.g., to build a molecular network or find structural analogues beyond cosine-based matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-pairwise-similarity-computation))
- **msms-pattern-interpretation**: Use when a GNPS molecular network component or spectral cluster needs a chemical interpretation, and MS/MS spectra (e.g., LC-HRMS .mgf with metadata) can be mined for shared, graph-structured m/z differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-pattern-interpretation))
- **msms-peak-filtering**: Use when raw MS/MS peak sets from spectra in a molecular network component must be preprocessed before MMSA multiple spectral alignment, e.g. to reduce noise and outlier intensity effects prior to consensus peak identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-filtering))
- **msms-peak-formula-annotation-matching**: Use when comparing predicted formula annotations against expert-curated peak annotations on held-out MS/MS spectra (e.g., NIST20 scaffold-split test set) to score formula-level agreement of C2MS models such as FraGNNet, GrAFF-MS, or ICEBERG. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-formula-annotation-matching))
- **msms-peak-fragment-matching**: Use when you have a measured HR-MS/MS peak list and a set of candidate structures, and need to rank candidates by how well their in silico fragments explain the product ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-fragment-matching))
- **msms-peak-intensity-coverage-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify how much of the total peak intensity of real MS/MS spectra (e.g., NIST20, [M+H]+, HCD Orbitrap) is explained by an approximate fragmentation DAG with given depth d and hydrogen tolerance j. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-intensity-coverage-analysis))
- **msms-peak-list-extraction**: Use when a raw MS/MS spectrum loaded from an MGF file must have low relative-intensity peaks removed via matchms, and you need the retained and filtered-out peak lists for inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-list-extraction))
- **msms-peak-list-format-conversion**: Use when raw MS/MS contest or experiment peak lists (precursor m/z plus fragment m/z-intensity pairs, possibly at several collision energies) must be restructured into MAGMa hierarchical input files before in silico candidate annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-list-format-conversion))
- **msms-peak-list-processing**: Use when LC-MS/MS runs in mzML or mzXML format must be converted into cleaned, feature-merged peak lists feeding molecular formula determination or structure annotation with SIRIUS 4 / COSMIC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-list-processing))
- **msms-peak-list-scoring**: Use when open mass spectral library records (.MSP/.MGF/.JSON/.CSV/.XML) contain inconsistent, oversized, or low-information peak lists that must be cleaned and scored before library integration or MS/MS annotation in MS-Dial. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-list-scoring))
- **msms-peak-post-processing-filtering**: Use when a predicted ESI-MS/MS peak list (CFM-ID 4.0/3.0/2.0 output at 10, 20, 40 eV) must be trimmed to a canonical form before comparing against experimental Metlin QTOF spectra with peak-match metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-post-processing-filtering))
- **msms-peak-presence-thresholding**: Use when reconstructing in silico MS/MS spectra from candidate SMILES with SingleFrag: for each of the most frequented MZ positions, an ANN prediction must be converted into a binary peak call via a position-specific threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-presence-thresholding))
- **msms-peak-substructure-matching**: Use when annotating unknown fragment ion spectra solely from candidate structures in the absence of reference spectra, e.g. ranking PubChem candidates for LC-MS metabolite identification challenges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-peak-substructure-matching))
- **msms-precursor-formula-ranking**: Use when a precursor ion's molecular formula must be ranked from an LC-MS/MS spectrum using fragment peak masses and a PubChem-derived formula list, without MS/MS databases or trained models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-precursor-formula-ranking))
- **msms-precursor-fragment-association**: Use when processing high-dimensional LC-IMS-MS/MS data where MS2 fragment ions must be attributed to MS1 precursors, especially when retention time alone is insufficient to separate coeluting species and drift time must be jointly considered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-precursor-fragment-association))
- **msms-precursor-mass-filtering**: Use when cleaning raw MS/MS spectra prior to formula identification — e.g., converting m/z to monoisotopic Da via adduct correction and removing spurious precursor-adjacent and contamination peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-precursor-mass-filtering))
- **msms-precursor-mass-matching**: Use when MS/MS spectra from untargeted metabolomics have no level 1 hit from an authentic-standard library and you need MSI level 2 annotations from public or in-silico spectral libraries without retention time confirmation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-precursor-mass-matching))
- **msms-predict**: Predict MS/MS spectra and fragment assignments from SMILES with GLACIER or ICEBERG, and score supplied candidate structures against experimental spectra. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-predict) · ★3)
- **msms-preprocess**: Convert raw instrument files (.raw, .d, etc.) to mzML or MGF for downstream model input; no-op if the file is already in a supported format. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-preprocess) · ★3)
- **msms-product-ion-intensity-normalization**: Use when assessing day-to-day MS/MS product ion consistency from DDA-acquired system suitability (SST) data where stochastic precursor triggering makes raw product ion intensities incomparable across scans and acquisitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-product-ion-intensity-normalization))
- **msms-product-ion-quantification**: Use when LC-MS/MS mzML data (e.g., deuterated acylcarnitine SST mixture from MassIVE MSV000098825) must be queried for specific product ion intensities or neutral-loss signals across NCE steps and activation modes (CID vs HCD). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-product-ion-quantification))
- **msms-query-spectrum-processing**: Use when MS/MS query spectra from an annotated molecular network (GNPS/MassIVE) need per-spectrum molecular formula and structure identification, or when benchmarking a new annotation method against SIRIUS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-query-spectrum-processing))
- **msms-retention-score-integration**: Use when candidate lists from MS/MS tools (SIRIUS, MS-DIAL) for LC-MS peaks in an RPLC run need re-ranking with predicted retention-order scores, or when peaks lack usable MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-retention-score-integration))
- **msms-scan-retention-time-filtering**: Use when processing ddMS2-topN or AIF UHPLC-HRMS/MS .ms2 files after MS1 exact-mass matching: you need to isolate the tandem-MS scans co-eluting with each feature before matching experimental fragments against in silico library m/z values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-scan-retention-time-filtering))
- **msms-scan-statistics-aggregation**: Use when a feature table row has multiple lipid candidates passing rule-based identification (same or similar m/z, co-eluting) and you must rank them by summed fragment intensity across MS/MS scans spanning the feature peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-scan-statistics-aggregation))
- **msms-scan-to-peak-group-assignment**: Use when processing LC-MS/MS metabolomics or lipidomics mzML samples where fragmentation scans must be grouped into precursor-defined (m/z, RT) slices and attached to finalized peak groups rather than analyzed per-scan. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-scan-to-peak-group-assignment))
- **msms-search-result-file-parsing**: Use when X!Tandem XML output files (e.g. from thegpm.org search engine) must be read programmatically to extract identifications, or when parsing-time/memory performance of search-result parsers is being benchmarked. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/msms-search-result-file-parsing))
- **msms-search-score-interpretation**: Use when input is an X!Tandem XML result file and you need per-spectrum identification details (peptide sequence, modified sequence, E-value, hyperscore, protein accession, charge, precursor and fragment m/z) exported for scoring interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/msms-search-score-interpretation))
- **msms-setup**: Install these skills, configure local, SSH, Slurm or Kubernetes prediction servers, tune each model host in configs/local.yaml, and register the MCP server. Use when asked to install or set up the skills, or when model hosts change. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-setup) · ★3)
- **msms-shifted-peak-alignment-analysis**: Use when two structurally related MS/MS spectra (MS2-known and MS2-unknown) are available with a 2D structure of the known compound, and the goal is to localize where the structural modification occurs on the known molecule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-shifted-peak-alignment-analysis))
- **msms-similarity-network-cluster-visualization**: Use when exploring LC-MS/MS .mgf feature spectra with specXplore and you want cluster groupings (at several k values) of features based on ms2deepscore similarities, not edge-threshold-driven partial networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-similarity-network-cluster-visualization))
- **msms-similarity-network-propagation**: Use when an LC-MS/MS peak table and MS2 spectra contain library-confirmed known metabolites and reaction-paired unknown peaks that must be annotated by propagating known seeds across spectrally similar, knowledge-guided network edges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-similarity-network-propagation))
- **msms-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have per-sample MS-DIAL feature lists (precursor MS1 m/z, RT, MS/MS spectra) from many samples (e.g. 545 wild soybean accessions) and need to align and merge equivalent features into one table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-similarity-scoring))
- **msms-spectra-annotation**: Use when experimental HR-MS/MS spectra (MGF) must be classified into user-defined substance classes, e.g. prioritizing putative (prenylated) flavonoids in natural product discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-annotation))
- **msms-spectra-binning-and-feature-extraction**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra (MoNA or NIST 17, positive mode, HCD/QqQ/Q-TOF or IT) must be preprocessed into binned, denoised feature vectors to train a multi-class multi-label ANN like MetFID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-binning-and-feature-extraction))
- **msms-spectra-canopus-class-prediction**: Use when per-spectrum or molecular-family-level chemical class annotations (ClassyFire/NPClassifier at super-, class-, and subclass-levels) are needed from MS/MS spectra in an NPLinker workflow and compounds are below 850 Da. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-canopus-class-prediction))
- **msms-spectra-compound-class-prediction**: Use when experimental HR-MS/MS spectra (MGF files) must be screened against a user-defined 'relevant' substance class (e.g. prenylated flavonoids) for prioritization, and you have curated 'relevant' and 'other' reference spectra to train classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-compound-class-prediction))
- **msms-spectra-csv-ingestion**: Use when you have four comma-separated CSV peak lists of MS/MS spectra — [M+H]+ and [M-H]- at 35% and 130% NCE, with m/z in column 1 and intensity in column 2 — and need spectra embeddings for downstream structure decoding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-csv-ingestion))
- **msms-spectra-database-searching**: Use when LC-MS/MS spectra of metabolites or other small molecules must be identified at the structure level by searching a molecular structure database such as PubChem, and measured spectral-library matching (e.g. GNPS library cosine search) is insufficient or unavailable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-database-searching))
- **msms-spectra-export-mgf-mat**: Use when detected LC-MS features have associated MS/MS spectra and you need portable spectral files (MGF/MAT) for GNPS spectral-library dereplication, MS-FINDER, or Sirius annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-export-mgf-mat))
- **msms-spectra-library-curation**: Use when a local, self-contained reference compound/MS2 spectral database (e.g. SQLite CompDb) must be created from a public resource like HMDB so experimental fragment spectra can later be compared against a curated library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-library-curation))
- **msms-spectra-merging**: Use when tandem MS/MS spectra must be converted into merged training feature vectors for per-bit libSVM fingerprint prediction, and candidate compounds must be ranked by Jaccard similarity of predicted vs. reference fingerprints from chemdb. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-merging))
- **msms-spectra-merging-and-renormalization**: Use when two centroided MS/MS peak lists (m/z, intensity) must be combined into a merged spectrum AB to compute spectral entropy similarity, e.g. for library matching or compound identification scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-merging-and-renormalization))
- **msms-spectra-merging-by-inchikey**: Use when building a training set from MS/MS spectral libraries (e.g. NIST 17, MoNA) where the same compound appears in multiple spectra at different collision energies or instrument types, and a single unified input vector per compound is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-merging-by-inchikey))
- **msms-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when preparing raw MGF spectra from PRIDE-style datasets (e.g., PXD000561 charge 2/3 MS/MS) for spectral clustering and you need reproducible peak filtering, intensity normalization, and low-quality spectrum removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-preprocessing--c2b51c05a721c4e7))
- **msms-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when input is raw MSP files of tandem MS spectra (possibly split across multiple collision energies) and you need model-ready spectra arrays for CNN-based metabolite identification with ChemEmbed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-preprocessing--0b989b8857a69781))
- **msms-spectra-reconstruction-from-per-position-predictions**: Use when you have per-MZ-position peak predictions (one model per MZ bin, each outputting a presence score) and need to assemble whole predicted MS/MS spectra for a molecule or library of molecules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-reconstruction-from-per-position-predictions))
- **msms-spectra-retrieval-from-mona**: Use when a standards dataframe row contains an inchiKey (linking to MassBank of North America) and you want to enrich an exported LC-MS reference library with MoNA MS/MS spectra alongside the adduct RT/m/z and quality fields. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-retrieval-from-mona))
- **msms-spectra-scaffold-retrieval-ranking**: Use when a de novo molecular structure generation pipeline needs to select a molecular scaffold from a candidate set given an MS/MS spectrum, either predictively (contrastive ranking) or via an oracle scaffold lookup keyed by spectrum and chemical formula. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-scaffold-retrieval-ranking))
- **msms-spectra-sirius-csi-fingerid-annotation**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS spectra (e.g. HILIC- or CSH-Q Exactive HF data) cannot be matched to spectral libraries at MSI level 1/2 and in silico structure candidates must be ranked for MSI level 3 annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-sirius-csi-fingerid-annotation))
- **msms-spectra-tokenization-for-transformer-training**: Use when reference MS/MS library spectra in NIST .msp format (e.g. compiled from MoNA/GNPS in ESI+ or ESI−) need to be converted into tokenized source/target sequences to train a transformer that predicts fingerprint descriptors or SMILES. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectra-tokenization-for-transformer-training))
- **msms-spectral-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when you have model-predicted fingerprints for unlabeled MS/MS spectra (e.g., .msp-derived ESI+/ESI− predictions) plus a reference fingerprint database (LipidMaps) and need to quantify top-hit annotation accuracy, overall and stratified by lipid class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-annotation-benchmarking))
- **msms-spectral-batch-search-against-public-repositories**: Use when you have multiple MS/MS spectra (e.g. a .mgf from MZmine feature-based networking, or a USI .csv/.tsv list) and want to find which public datasets and taxonomic lineages they match. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-batch-search-against-public-repositories))
- **msms-spectral-binarization**: Use when preparing MS/MS spectra for per-fragment binary prediction: instead of regressing peak intensities, spectra are binarized by m/z bin and a separate model predicts presence/absence per bin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-binarization))
- **msms-spectral-binning-feature-engineering**: Use when preparing MS/MS spectra (MoNA, NIST 17, HCD/QqQ/Q-TOF or IT, positive mode) as neural-network input vectors for fingerprint prediction, and a uniform binning/scaling representation is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-binning-feature-engineering))
- **msms-spectral-cosine-scoring**: Use when ranking candidate metabolite annotations from predicted vs. experimental tandem mass spectra, or when judging whether the top-ranked annotation is trustworthy via the Δcos gap between top-1 and top-2 candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-cosine-scoring))
- **msms-spectral-count-verification**: Use when deconvoluted MS/MS spectra have been exported to .msp format from raw LC-MS/MS data and you must validate that the number of unique spectra per ionization mode matches reported/published counts before downstream modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-count-verification))
- **msms-spectral-data-format-conversion**: Use when raw benchmark MS/MS files must be converted to mgf with title, precursor_mz, precursor_type and collision_energy fields so FIDDLE (or SIRIUS/BUDDY baselines) can consume them for formula-identification evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-data-format-conversion))
- **msms-spectral-database-retrieval**: Use when when you have a target compound (modified or unmodified) and need to obtain its experimental MS/MS spectrum and metadata to serve as a known reference for ModiFinder analysis, or when benchmarking evaluation methods like average_distance scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msms-spectral-database-retrieval) · 2 copies)
- **msms-spectral-dataset-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra (MGF, mzML, msp, JSON from GNPS or similar libraries) must be cleaned and harmonized before training spectral embeddings, computing cosine/modified-cosine similarities, or building molecular networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-dataset-preprocessing))
- **msms-spectral-denoising-parameter-tuning**: Use when MS/MS query spectra were acquired on a non-Orbitrap high-resolution analyzer and the NIST23-derived electronic denoising threshold and mass tolerances must be re-derived and validated before applying Spectral Denoising. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-denoising-parameter-tuning))
- **msms-spectral-dot-product-scoring**: Use when experimental MS/MS peak lists from small-molecule metabolomics must be matched against candidate chemical structures to produce ranked, calibrated exact-structure assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-dot-product-scoring))
- **msms-spectral-embedding-generation**: Use when HR-MS/MS spectra (e.g., MGF-format annotated spectra from MSnLib or in-house libraries) must be converted into MS2DeepScore feature vectors before training or applying spectra classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-embedding-generation))
- **msms-spectral-fingerprint-construction**: Use when query MS/MS spectra (validated reference spectra or cryptic spectra without GNPS library matches) must be converted into per-sample similarity vectors over paired genome-MS/MS samples so a KNN classifier can assign gene cluster family (GCF) labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-fingerprint-construction))
- **msms-spectral-library-analogue-search**: Use when you have cleaned positive- or negative-mode MS2 query spectra (mzML, MGF, msp, mzXML, json, USI, or pickled matchms) and need reliable library matches and analogues beyond modified cosine search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-analogue-search))
- **msms-spectral-library-curation**: Use when batch QC inspection of LC-MS DDA data (e.g., TripleTOF or QE HF converted Aird files) has produced checked analysis overviews and you need to build a homemade MS/MS spectral library from inspected-and-passed compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-curation))
- **msms-spectral-library-filtering**: Use when building training data for MS/MS spectrum classification: you have MGF spectral library files and SMARTS structure queries for a compound class of interest and need to label spectra as 'relevant' or 'other'. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-filtering))
- **msms-spectral-library-matching**: Use when chemical annotations must be assigned to MS/MS spectra in public MassIVE files or one's own data by spectral reference matching (GNPS library search), or when building a binary annotation-by-file matrix from ReDU's pre-computed results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-matching--bbcd41e49d425d46))
- **msms-spectral-library-matching**: Use when detected LC-MS features with acquired MS/MS spectra (mzML runs processed in Optimus) need putative molecular annotations via MS/MS library matching on GNPS, or mz-RT matching to a GNPS-exported molecule list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-matching--6d9ae2670ff03a11))
- **msms-spectral-library-search**: Use when you have experimental metabolomics MS/MS spectra (e.g. MGF-format human blood spectra) and want to count how many can be annotated by reference-library search, comparing coverage across multiple candidate libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-search--e1438f6b6ab54f53))
- **msms-spectral-library-search**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS feature spectra (mzML/mgf from raw vendor files) must be annotated against a drug reference library to detect parent drugs, metabolized analogs, or drug exposures in biological or environmental samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-search--c812b8504cb3f4e3))
- **msms-spectral-library-searching**: Use when a large set of experimental MS/MS spectra needs structural annotation via cosine spectral matching against an in silico predicted library (e.g. HMDB + DeepMet candidates) rather than direct formula assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-searching--503bde62c7a55504))
- **msms-spectral-library-searching**: Use when LC-MS/MS spectra (mzML) from metabolomics or lipidomics samples must be matched against a fragmentation spectral library and identifications ranked or scored for precision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-searching--15c9f4ddabcfdac9))
- **msms-spectral-library-searching**: Use when consensus MS2 spectra already associated with precursor m/z and retention time (e.g. deconvolved DIA data or DDA pooled QC spectra) must be annotated with named chemical identities via spectral library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-library-searching--e8a9c6a849ad484b))
- **msms-spectral-matching**: Use when MS/MS peak lists (.mgf) from natural product extracts must be annotated against a spectral database, or when establishing a pre-taxonomic baseline of rank-1 correct annotations for tools like ISDB-DNP, Sirius, or MS-Finder. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-matching))
