- **msms-spectral-mst-based-multiple-alignment**: Use when a molecular networking component contains multiple tandem mass spectra (e.g., adducts or in-source fragments of one metabolite) and you need to identify which consensus peaks drive the family's shared fragmentation beyond pairwise spectral comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-mst-based-multiple-alignment))
- **msms-spectral-noise-perturbation**: Use when evaluating robustness of formula-identification methods on MS/MS spectra (MGF format) by systematically injecting intensity noise and comparing top-k accuracy degradation across instruments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-noise-perturbation))
- **msms-spectral-pair-alignment**: Use when a pair of tandem MS spectra from structurally related analogs must be peak-aligned to identify which fragment peaks are shifted between the MS2-known and MS2-unknown spectra, prior to modification-site localization or analog-pair filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-pair-alignment))
- **msms-spectral-peak-presence-prediction**: Use when you need in silico MS/MS spectra for metabolites lacking empirical library entries, or when predicting binary peak presence at specific m/z bins (2-decimal resolution) is preferred over intensity regression across collision energies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-peak-presence-prediction))
- **msms-spectral-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra from spectral libraries (e.g., MoNA or NIST 17) need to be cleaned, merged per compound, and converted into integer m/z bin feature vectors before training or applying a machine-learning metabolite annotation model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-preprocessing))
- **msms-spectral-preprocessing-filtering**: Use when preparing MS/MS spectra for MMSA or multiple spectral alignment in a molecular family — before building the minimum spanning tree, topological sorting, and transitive alignment steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-preprocessing-filtering--774c70e074281649))
- **msms-spectral-preprocessing-filtering**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra acquired across multiple collision energies must be cleaned (noise peak removal, invalid fragment removal) before computing per-fragment LFP intensity vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-preprocessing-filtering--05224c5767d43ff1))
- **msms-spectral-search**: Use when a molecular feature of interest from an untargeted metabolomics dataset is unannotated or only Level 2/3 annotated, and you want to map it to prior public MS/MS data from specific tissues, organisms, or disease cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-search))
- **msms-spectral-similarity-computation**: Use when you have a processed LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics feature table with associated MS/MS spectra and need to quantify structural similarity between every feature pair before clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-similarity-computation))
- **msms-spectral-similarity-molecular-networking**: Use when you have untargeted LC-MS/MS data (converted to open formats like .mzXML/.mzML/.mgf) and want to group related molecules, dereplicate knowns via library matching, and propagate annotations across a spectral network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-similarity-molecular-networking))
- **msms-spectral-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra of an unknown metabolite (e.g. from Shimadzu LC-ESI-IT-TOF or Bruker Compact LC-ESI-QTOF) and need to identify it against a database whose entries may only be structurally related, not identical, compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-similarity-scoring))
- **msms-spectral-tokenization**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra in .msp format must be converted into transformer-ready token sequences (m/z binned into tokens, intensities normalized) for training or inference with IDSL_MINT-style encoder-decoder models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-tokenization))
- **msms-spectral-topic-modeling**: Use when you have a GNPS molecular network and want to discover recurring substructures in MS/MS spectra via LDA topic modeling, then map those Mass2Motifs back onto the network to annotate molecular families. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectral-topic-modeling))
- **msms-spectrum-alignment**: Use when you have MS2 spectra of a known library hit and an unknown analog whose precursor masses differ by a partial modification, and you need to determine which atoms/substructures carry the modification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-alignment))
- **msms-spectrum-annotation**: Use when given a precursor peak with its MS/MS fragment peak list (composite spectra across collision energies) and no reference spectra, and candidate structures must be ranked by how well each explains the observed fragments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-annotation))
- **msms-spectrum-clustering**: Use when input is a collection of MS/MS spectra (mzXML/mzML from LC-MS/MS runs, e.g. deposited in MassIVE) and the goal is to cluster related spectra into a node/edge molecular network for dereplication or analog discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-clustering))
- **msms-spectrum-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when quantifying the efficiency of a spectrum clustering tool on large MS/MS datasets — e.g. comparing nearest-neighbor search cost (~47 comparisons/spectrum for falcon) to exhaustive pairwise comparison within a 20 ppm precursor tolerance, or timing clustering of millions of spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-clustering-benchmarking))
- **msms-spectrum-data-structure-manipulation**: Use when you have raw or downloaded MSMS spectra (from online resources via Universal Spectrum Identifier, or from local mzML/mzXML files) that require standardization, cleaning, and fragment assignment before visualization, statistical comparison, or machine-learning feature extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msms-spectrum-data-structure-manipulation))
- **msms-spectrum-deconvolution-export**: Use when deconvoluted MS/MS peak lists from LC-MS/MS metabolomics data (e.g. Q Exactive HF acquisitions) must be exported as .mgf files to feed downstream spectral library searches such as the NIST17 hybrid search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-deconvolution-export))
- **msms-spectrum-document-conversion**: Use when processed MS/MS spectra (matchms Spectrum objects from MGF/JSON such as the AllPositive deposit at zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.3979010) must be tokenized into peak and loss word documents to train a Spec2Vec Word2Vec model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-document-conversion))
- **msms-spectrum-extraction-from-molecular-network**: Use when unannotated MS/MS features in a molecular-network subnetwork need their consensus spectra exported (e.g. as .mgf or .ms) for in-silico classification by tools like SIRIUS, starting from public GNPS/MassIVE untargeted metabolomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-extraction-from-molecular-network))
- **msms-spectrum-feature-encoding**: Use when reimplementing, validating, or extending GLEAMS's spectrum encoder, or when converting raw MS/MS spectra (precursor mass, charge, peak lists) into the exact 3010-feature input format its neural network expects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-feature-encoding))
- **msms-spectrum-file-parsing**: Use when input spectra are CSV files for four experimental conditions — precursor [M+H]+ or [M-H]- at 35% NCE or 130% NCE — that must be formatted (m/z column 1, intensity column 2, comma-separated) before feeding the Spec2Mol encoder via predict_embs.py. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-file-parsing))
- **msms-spectrum-formula-ranking**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra (positive mode, NPLIB1/NIST20-style) with mass-enumerated formula candidates and need to downselect to a tractable top-k set that retains the true formula. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-formula-ranking))
- **msms-spectrum-fragment-formula-assignment**: Use when assigning/ranking molecular formulas for deposited LC-MS/MS spectra (e.g., HMDB or NPL datasets) with dataset-specific mass-matching tolerances and the top-30 most-abundant fragment peaks, without relying on MS/MS spectral databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-fragment-formula-assignment))
- **msms-spectrum-metadata-preservation**: Use when removing invalid or malformed entries (e.g., SMILES validation, format errors) from large spectral datasets (GNPS, MoNA, MTBLS1572, MassBank). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msms-spectrum-metadata-preservation) · 2 copies)
- **msms-spectrum-noise-filtering**: Use when an MS/MS spectrum (e.g. loaded from a USI such as mzspec:PXD004732:...:scan:41840) contains precursor peaks, noise peaks, or overly intense peaks that must be removed or de-emphasized before fragment annotation or publication-quality plotting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-noise-filtering))
- **msms-spectrum-object-instantiation**: Use when when you have successfully resolved a USI string to extract raw spectrum data from an online proteomics/metabolomics repository (PRIDE, MassIVE, etc.) and need to construct a spectrum object that exposes spectrum data as Python attributes and methods for downstream processing (filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/msms-spectrum-object-instantiation))
- **msms-spectrum-pair-feature-encoding**: Use when MS/MS spectra of an unknown metabolite must be converted into paired feature vectors against database candidate structures so a trained similarity-scoring model can rank candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-pair-feature-encoding))
- **msms-spectrum-peak-filtering**: Use when raw GNPS or other MS/MS library spectra (e.g. ALL_GNPS.json) must be peak-filtered before spectral similarity scoring, model training, or building annotated training subsets like AllPositive/UniqueInchikey. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-peak-filtering))
- **msms-spectrum-peak-normalization**: Use when an experimental MS/MS spectrum (peak m/z and intensity pairs) must be scored against theoretical fragment peak lists from candidate molecules, and intensity-scale differences between spectra would bias the match score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-peak-normalization))
- **msms-spectrum-prediction**: Use when you need in silico MS/MS spectra for compounds with known SMILES (e.g., CASMI challenge sets, [M+H]+/[M-H]- precursors) and must quantify prediction quality via cosine similarity versus annotated peak-intensity coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-prediction--ae2a8db23c77b0e6))
- **msms-spectrum-prediction**: Use when you have molecule SMILES but no experimental MS/MS reference spectrum, and you need predicted spectra for library generation, metabolite annotation, or benchmarking against tools like CFM-ID and 3DMolMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-prediction--cdbb241a6fab9749))
- **msms-spectrum-prediction-inference**: Use when a trained C2MS model must convert a molecular structure into a predicted tandem MS/MS spectrum — e.g. to predict spectra for PubChem molecules absent from NIST20 so they can be ranked against an unknown spectrum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-prediction-inference))
- **msms-spectrum-prediction-spectral-angle-calculation**: Use when rescoring peptide-spectrum matches from immunopeptidomics searches (pep.xml + MGF inputs) and you want a peptide-specific, search-engine-independent quality feature based on predicted-vs-experimental spectral similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/msms-spectrum-prediction-spectral-angle-calculation))
- **msms-spectrum-prediction-training**: Use when a task requires simulating MS/MS spectra from a molecular structure — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-prediction-training))
- **msms-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when input is a raw MSP file of MS/MS peak lists (e.g. Orbitrap or QTOF scans at several collision energies) that must be turned into fixed-width model inputs for embedding prediction or candidate matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-preprocessing--574a54e3ab5cf42a))
- **msms-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS data (or MZmine output) must be converted to .mzXML, MS2 centroided, exported as MS2-only .csv plus .mgf spectra, and library-matched in GNPS2 FBMN to reproduce cosine/paired-peak annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-preprocessing--ae03a738007ecf6d))
- **msms-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when preparing raw or centroided query spectra and spectral library spectra (e.g. iPRG2012 TripleTOF data, NIST/Selevsek libraries) as input to ANN-SoLo or SpectraST-style library searching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-preprocessing--40d3c24b75a52942))
- **msms-spectrum-preprocessing-binning**: Use when raw MS/MS peak lists (precursor m/z, ionization mode, m/z-intensity pairs) must be converted into MetFID's fixed-length binned feature vectors before loading them into the fingerprint-prediction ANN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-preprocessing-binning))
- **msms-spectrum-preprocessing-peak-filtering**: Use when raw or externally sourced MS/MS spectra must be prepared as inputs for MADGEN (or a similar spectra-conditioned generative model) and are not already included in the preprocessed Zenodo dataset pickles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-preprocessing-peak-filtering--49762f467e5685b9))
- **msms-spectrum-preprocessing-peak-filtering**: Use when raw MS/MS peak files (mzML, mzXML, or MGF) must be cleaned before spectrum similarity computation, feature hashing, or clustering — e.g. precursor peaks and noise dominate the spectrum and would distort cosine similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-preprocessing-peak-filtering--5e73a080de897e7e))
- **msms-spectrum-quality-metrics**: Use when antibody MGF spectra plus a database-search pseudo-ground truth (MS-GF+/X!Tandem, 1% FDR) are available and you need to quantify missing fragment ions or noise-peak fractions to interpret de novo sequencing errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-quality-metrics))
- **msms-spectrum-relevant-other-labeling**: Use when HR-MS/MS spectra of novel compounds must be labeled 'relevant' vs 'other' for a user-defined substance class (e.g., prenylated (iso-)flavonoids) from annotated training spectra and MS2DeepScore embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-relevant-other-labeling))
- **msms-spectrum-scoring-with-deep-neural-network**: Use when an unknown metabolite's MS/MS spectrum must be matched to a structurally related database entry (e.g. KEGG compounds), because no exact reference spectrum exists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-scoring-with-deep-neural-network))
- **msms-spectrum-vectorization**: Use when filtered MS/MS spectra (matchms Spectrum objects or m/z + intensity arrays) must be turned into fixed-width numerical vectors — e.g., to feed a Siamese neural network for embedding creation or pairwise similarity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-spectrum-vectorization))
- **msms-structure-elucidation-skills**: Coordinate end-to-end MS/MS molecular structure elucidation from spectra, feature exports, raw data or public dataset links through candidate search, spectral prediction, evidence review and an interactive report. Use when asked to elucidate unknown structures or run this skill collection. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-structure-elucidation-skills) · ★3)
- **msms-structure-proposal**: Propose new candidate SMILES from matched and unexplained MS/MS peaks, record the structural reasoning, and validate formulas before handing candidates to msms-predict for scoring. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-structure-proposal) · ★3)
- **msms-subformulae**: Assign per-peak molecular subformulae to an MS/MS spectrum, producing the peakformula features required by FRIGID's MIST encoder. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-subformulae) · ★3)
- **msms-synthetic-noise-simulation**: Use when validating a denoising pipeline on clean query MS/MS spectra: synthetic chemical noise (from formula_db) and electronic noise must be injected before running spectral_denoising and scoring recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-synthetic-noise-simulation))
- **msms-transition-pair-extraction**: Use when a curated tandem MS library (msp format, e.g. from mspcompiler output) must be converted into pseudo-MRM transition tables for MRMPROBS so chemicals already identified in DIA data can be sensitively re-screened without re-acquisition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-transition-pair-extraction))
- **msms-validated-annotation-matching**: Use when comparing metabolite annotation outputs (formula+adduct pairs at a stated FDR, e.g. &lt; 10%) from two or more annotation methods against an independent MS/MS-validated bulk LC-MS/MS reference ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msms-validated-annotation-matching))
- **msms-visualize**: Open a local interactive webpage for ranked MS/MS spectra, fragment structures, and saved candidate review notes after retrieval or review. ([coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills](https://github.com/coleygroup/msms-structure-elucidation.skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/msms-visualize) · ★3)
- **msmtp**: Use this skill when the user wants to send an email via any SMTP server, send notifications via email, or deliver mail programmatically. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/msmtp/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **msn**: Use when browsing the Minato, Shimanto, and Nagare workflow hierarchy through the HASHI flow registry commands. ([Bazza1982/HASHI](https://github.com/Bazza1982/HASHI/tree/HEAD/skills/msn) · ★1)
- **msn-fragment-tree-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have multiple candidate SMILES structures from a de novo prediction step and a low-resolution MS2/MSn peak list, and need to rank candidates by comparing simulated fragmentation trees against the tree built from experimental peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msn-fragment-tree-similarity-scoring))
- **msn-js-sdk**: Integrates the MSN JavaScript SDK (`window.$msstart`) into JavaScript games running on Microsoft Start. Handles SDK initialization, player authentication, cloud save key/value storage, display banner and interstitial/rewarded ad lifecycles, native sharing, leaderboard score submission, and in-app purchases. Use when the user mentions MSN SDK setup, MSN ads integration, game monetization with MSN, adding MSN features to a JavaScript game, `$msstart`, `msstart` script, MSN cloud save, or MSN IAP. ([popey/gamedev.sh-skills](https://github.com/popey/gamedev.sh-skills/tree/HEAD/msn-js-sdk))
- **msn-spectrum-file-parsing**: Use when the task requires reprocessing a deposited spectral library (e.g. MSnLib Zenodo 11163380) to reproduce reported totals such as 357,065 MS2 spectra, &gt;2.3 million merged MSn spectra, and 87% compound coverage over 30,008 unique small molecules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msn-spectrum-file-parsing))
- **msn-tree-construction**: Use when converting multi-stage fragmentation raw data (e.g. Orbitrap ID-X runs exported as mzML) into tree-structured MSn spectra with precursor-child relationships, prior to library export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msn-tree-construction))
- **msn-tree-fragmentation-annotation**: Use when building lipid spectral libraries or annotating lipid identifications from Thermo raw LC-MSn data where MS3+ spectra and tree-based fragment logic (e.g. required-fragment downgrades) are needed for structural confidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msn-tree-fragmentation-annotation))
- **msn-tree-fragmentation-method-design**: Use when building an in silico spectral library for novel or complex lipid classes (glycolipids, lipopeptides, FAHFA, cardiolipin) or tailoring fragmentation rules to a specific MS2/MS3+ acquisition method in LipiDex 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/msn-tree-fragmentation-method-design))
- **msnn-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to predict the immediate next navigation step in a 3D scene given a multi-modal situation description and a textual goal.
